金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 09 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 期中選舉「藍色浪潮」，AI 基建政策風險升溫：11 月 3 日美國期中選舉民主黨奪回參眾兩院，
德州新任州長揚言暫停資料中心建設，市場擔憂電力、用水與環境審查趨嚴，AI 資本支出前景
遭重新評價；加上 Fed 官員釋出續升息訊號，資金撤出高評價半導體與 AI 基建族群，美股科
技股大幅修正。短線政策雜音難消，但 AI 需求本質未變，後續觀察新國會實際立法方向。
◼ 科技財報喜憂參半，台股賣壓沉重、政府出手穩市：蘋果營收優於預期但毛利率受記憶體成本

壓抑，AMD 資料中心營收近倍增、高通財報後走弱。美股賣壓延燒，台股連日重挫、新台幣
貶破 33 元，行政院指示金管會機動因應並研議穩定股市措施，官股行庫與投信持續買超。看
法：短線波動仍大，但基本面與政策支撐有助指數逐步築底。
◼ 美國製造業續擴張，台灣通膨降溫：美國 10 月 ISM 製造業指數升至 55.5，連續第十個月擴
張，惟價格分項仍處高檔；台灣 10 月製造業 PMI 連續 13 個月擴張但速度趨緩，CPI 年增率
降至 2.33%。看法：美國景氣與通膨黏性支撐 Fed 偏鷹立場，台灣央行壓力相對減輕；後續
關注 10 月非農就業、11 月 10 日美國 CPI 及 12 月 FOMC 會議。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，
惟短線宜重視個股分化，美國期中選舉帶來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，
惟大型雲端業者持續投入 AI 基礎建設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，科技股整
體基本面仍具支撐，但短期應避免過度集中於高評價、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企

業。優先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業。
◼ 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價調整期
間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延
長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置
穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人
仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

### Page 3

3
Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 政治風險, 聽證會與法案: 黃仁勳赴國會及參議院聽證, 暗示短期難以回歸; 年底NDAA法案包裹一系列
晶片相關限制等小法案, 恐進一步壓抑海外需求並加速供應鏈分化。
• 輝達量化緊縮, 連鎖效應擴散: 輝達明年營收增長率至75%, 且採購承諾將如期進行。不過新興雲端與
規模較小公司恐面臨較嚴格融資擔保條件。
• Fed助美元, 亞幣呈分化: 國際美元強勢, 但亞幣走出自己的路, 出口暢旺卻強弱分歧, 台日雙疲, 中韓強
勢, 估計該現象將延續至年底。
2. 後續看點:
• APEC會談結果(11/20)、美國PCE(11/25), OPEC+會議(11/29)。
策略思維
1) 股市−增速放緩, 但成長格局未變: 市場對AI投資效益及資料中心建置疑慮升溫, 科技股大幅修正。然而, 目
前主要瓶頸來自供給, 而非需求轉弱; 預計2028年EPS減速而不致負成長, 震盪放大但長多行情不致改變。
2) 債市−高利中樞環境成為常態: 升息預期、財政紀律及資金環境趨緊, 均將影響利率環境higher for longer; 惟

在市場波動加劇、高利優勢背景下, 固定收益資產配置價值仍高, 報酬以現金流為主。
3) 匯市−短線美元偏強震盪: 升息預期帶動美元指數走強, Fed官員偏鷹基調亦將對美元形成支撐。若未來地緣
溢價風險能延續降溫、油價走勢緩步回落, 將限縮美元指數大幅上行空間。

Cathay’s View
AI投資驅動半導體市場規模擴大 市場擔憂2028年投資放緩, 半導體銷售動能也將下滑
註: 8大CSP包括谷歌、微軟、亞馬遜、Meta、甲骨文、SpaceX、CoreWeave、Nebius。
2026年全球半導體銷售為今年前9月數據年化後結果。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/11/17。
• 民主黨勝選後, 市場開始擔憂AI監管政策可能影響資料中心興建進度, 如今輝達代理執行長又表示將更加審慎檢視
未來AI供應鏈投資方案, 進一步加深市場對AI資本支出放緩的疑慮。從半導體市場規模來看, 近年AI投資熱潮已帶
動產業快速擴張, 全球半導體銷售額從2025年的約7,250億美元, 預估攀升至2026年超過1.3兆美元, 顯示AI已成為
推動半導體產業成長的重要引擎。
• 然而, 市場真正擔憂的並非今年半導體需求是否持續成長, 而是2028年之後的成長動能能否延續。隨著大型CSP業
者逐步檢視資本支出效益, 加上資料中心興建可能受到融資成本與監管政策影響, 未來AI投資增速存在放緩風險,

進而使半導體銷售成長面臨壓力。
AI循環｜ AI投資驅動半導體版圖擴大, 惟市場擔心增速放緩
註: 8大CSP包括谷歌、微軟、亞馬遜、Meta、甲骨文、SpaceX、CoreWeave、Nebius。

• AI產業短線面臨諸多事件考驗：
• 產業焦點將轉向政策風險。黃仁勳赴國會及參議院聽證, 顯示AI監管與國安議題升溫; 年底NDAA法案包裹一系列
晶片相關限制小法案, 恐進一步壓抑海外需求並加速供應鏈分化。2027年新一屆國會上任後, 相關監管與管制措施

仍有加碼空間, 使AI產業面臨政策不確定性的逆風。

### Page 7

7
Cathay’s View
輝達的財務承諾及融資平台 市場擔心AI基建放緩, 股價評價下修
資料來源：Bloomberg,  截至2026/11/16。
• 輝達更換執行長後, 市場最關注的焦點之一, 就是先前承諾的AI相關融資計畫是否能夠持續推進, 包括已承諾的合
約, 以及規模高達5,000億美元的融資平台能否如期落實。一旦新任經營團隊對資本配置採取更審慎的態度, 相關
融資與投資承諾可能逐步放緩, 直接的影響就是資料中心的擴建速度將同步放緩。輝達最新財報中顯示, 財務承諾
金額從上季的3660億美元, 提高至5180億美元, 仍將如期進行。
• 對金融市場而言, 資料中心建置放緩, 也將導致市場下修AI需求的成長潛力, 估值也面臨同步下修的壓力, 後續將持
續關注新任執行長對投資策略及新國會後的政府動向。不過, 值得注意的是, 輝達放緩AI投資, 反而有助於輝達債
信品質的提升。
AI融資｜ 輝達目前仍維持既有融資策略, 但關注未來新任執行長的意向
資料來源：輝達財報Q3財報發布會, 截至2026/11/17。

### Page 8

8
Cathay’s View
• 新任執行長表示, 將對新興雲(Neocloud)給予「有紀律、具選擇性」的支持, 業者須先自行取得融資、完成算力部
署, 才能取得輝達320億美元的擔保承諾。預估2027年新興雲資本支出900-1000億美元, 其中CoreWeave和
Nebius預估明年的資本支出將達850億美元, 並已表示將透過客戶訂金、長約及GPU向銀行抵押融資, 取得7-8成
以上資金。其他新興雲規模較小, 即使無法取得輝達擔保承諾, 對明年整體高達1.5兆美元的AI資本支出影響有限。
• 輝達放緩AI投資, 對輝達營收和股票評價面是利空, 但對債信反而是正面影響, 輝達CDS在黃仁勳辭職後出現回落,
預期資金將流向大型CSP, 加速形成算力寡占市場。新興雲融資仰賴客戶長約與GPU抵押價值, 因此GPU租金維持
上漲、算力需求保持強勁成長, 將是未來能否降低AI融資風險的因素。
輝達新興雲擔保承諾及新興雲預估資本支出
資料來源: 輝達, Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027年, 截至2026/11/18。

Cathay’s View
台股評價面下調壓力沉重 資金flight-to-quality, 台積電亦遭大舉賣壓
註: 預估本益比=股價/未來一年預估EPS。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/11/18。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/18。
• Semi analysis大幅下修2028-2030年AI晶片資本支出3-4成, 同時CSP資本支出年增率預估在2028-2030年僅個位
數成長, 引發市場對半導體獲利預期下修, 並帶動更劇烈的估值下調, 台積電預估本益比從22倍下修至15倍, 台積
電以外的預估本益比從20倍修正至13倍, 使台股跌幅擴大。
• 此外, 市場恐慌情緒攀升, 資金轉向現金避險, 台積電亦遭拋售, 股市賣壓沉重但缺少低接買盤, 提高融資斷頭及多
殺多風險, 台股融資餘額從高點6400億元銳減至5080億元。

### Page 10

10
Cathay’s View
悲觀情境: 台積電2028年預估獲利從34%減至個位數 台積電啟動長期擴廠計畫, 反映對AI需求長期看好
註: 2027年預估EPS持平, 2028年下修15%。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027-2028年, 截至2026/11/18。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 預估範圍為2027-2028年, 截至2026/11/18。
• 市場對CSP資本支出增速放緩已形成初步共識, 並開始反映2028年半導體獲利下修風險。預估台積電2027年獲利
年增34%, 2028年受CSP投資放緩影響, EPS年增率將由26%降至個位數, 全年EPS預估160元。以11/19收盤價
2100元計算, 前瞻本益比僅約13倍, 處於歷史低檔, 股價拉回宜以買方思維看待。然而, 其他台股電子族群仍面臨
輝達供應鏈重組、零組件供需及漲價預期修正等壓力, 估值落底仍需時間, 短期震盪及反彈壓力較大。
• 台積電將赴美國德州擴廠, 並重申AI需求具備多年強勁成長動能, 反映對2030年後AI長期需求仍具信心。此外,

CSP於2028年適度放緩投資, 有助於延長AI產業成長週期, 並不代表半導體需求趨勢反轉。

近期聯準會官員談話 市場升息預期上修, 已積極預估至明年升逾3碼
註: 摘錄今年投票委員談話。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/11/18。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/17。
• 聯準會維持鷹派立場: 除理事Waller考慮12月升息50bps、聖路易斯Fed總裁Musalem支持2027年春季前多次升息
之外, 多數官員仍支持循序漸進的緊縮, 料12月升息1碼仍為基準情境。主席Warsh則探討AI發展的可能性, 儘管AI
效益轉化仍有待驗證, 但長期正向成長趨勢明確, 並將在未來一段期間內持續推升通膨壓力。
• 市場已提前反映未來升息空間: 現行市場預期Fed至2027年Q3累計升息3~4碼, 反映通膨黏著及經濟韌性。惟若能
源價格續跌或供需狀況緩步改善, 終端利率預估值或有調整空間。
• 高利環境持續成為常態: 通膨方面, 撇除地緣衝突影響外, 2027年AI投資動能仍將支撐經濟成長; 而美國財政赤字

及AI基礎建設再融資均推升公債/信用債供給量, 預期長天期利率續居高位。
美國利率｜官員談話延續鷹派立場, 升息預期持續支撐美債利率高檔

經濟、就業市場仍具韌性, 現階段通膨風險仍高, 而

AI發展情境推估:

3)AI發展未達預期, 引發股市下跌及經濟放緩。
Beth Hammack

ECB接班人傾向 2027/2歐美升息預期對比
資料來源: Econotream, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/11/17。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/10。
歐美匯率｜ECB接班對比Fed偏緊態度
• 拉加德人事變動短期雖不致改變ECB的年底升息路徑, 但因呼聲較高的接任者, 目前是偏鴿的前西班牙總裁, 加以
歐元區內部(法國預算)的金融穩定正成為限制ECB明年2月後持續升息的煞車。此外立場向來強硬的拉脫維亞總裁
卡扎克斯日前都開始強調「成長代價」與「微妙權衡」, 這向市場傳遞了一個重要訊號, 12月升息後, 利率將觸及
或略高於中性利率區間(1.75%～2.50%)上限, 2027年初若要再繼續升息, 門檻將會明顯提高。
• 對比美元受惠於美國第三季GDP成長3.4%的強勁韌性及黏著通膨, 支撐聯準會維持鷹派; 反觀歐元區第三季 GDP
僅增 0.4%, 加上法國債市風險, 歐美貨幣政策在2027年初的預期落差, 正壓抑短期歐元走勢, 支持美元高位偏強。

美元兌四國匯率走勢 四國出口年增率
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/17。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10。
• 中、韓、日、台四國近期受惠於全球貿易與AI科技需求, 出口表現普遍暢旺, 但匯率走勢卻呈現分化。
• 台日雙疲: 資金外流與政策掣肘而陷入「出口旺、匯率弱」的背離困境。新台幣受到外資在台股大舉獲利了結並
淨匯出百億美元, 即便台灣央行頻頻進場調節仍難敵龐大賣壓; 日圓則在160關卡前低檔徘徊, 由於日本第三季
GDP意外萎縮削弱升息底氣, 加上市場對日本財務省干預漸消, 美日利差套利交易再度壓抑日圓表現。
• 中韓雙強: 韓元稱霸主要受惠出口改善, 於韓國企業響應公司治理改革, 大舉實施庫藏股並將海外盈餘與貨款匯回
國內, 形成強勁的實質換匯需求; 人民幣則在出口高成長帶動下, 受惠於中國人民銀行順應市場供需「放手」調升
中間價, 而這些因素並非單一事件而是持續發生, 後續四個貨幣的走法也隨其持續機會較大。

元(預期1090億美元), GAAP EPS從2.46美元提高至2.70美元, 毛利率為74.8%, 均優於預期。展望2026年
Q4(2026/10/25-2027/1/31), 預期營收將達1300億美元, 不過受到記憶體價格上漲影響, 毛利率受到擠壓而下滑至
71.5%, 但後續有望改善。
• 從產品別來看, 資料中心仍是營收成長的主要驅動力, 從前期的890億美元上升至1076億美元, 年增率達110%, 營
收佔比維持在9成。另外, 輝達上修明年的營收成長預估, 從70%提高至75%。

美國零售銷售和控制組月增率 消費類股強弱與標普指數
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10, 虛線為滾動3ma。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/19。
美國消費股｜市場吹起避險風, 資金流向核心消費
• 近期核心零售展現韌性的背景下, 必需消費類股在當前市場中展現出優勢。全美汽油均價略下滑, 省下的可支配現
金重新導向日常剛性採買。帶動10月核心零售銷售月增0.6%、年增5.6%, 表現優於整體零售數據, 這些日用品等
領域為必需消費產業奠定基礎。
• 另一方面, 當前市場正處於風險趨避氛圍, 高估值AI科技成長股因資本支出檢視而震盪加劇, 在「基本面直接受惠
油價下跌」與「低Beta避險屬性」的雙重加持下, 消費類股成為當前進可攻、退可守的優勢配置。

阿里巴巴營收表現 中港股大盤與阿里巴巴股價走勢
中國｜阿里財報不如預期, 黃仁勳下台衝擊「外訓內推」模式, 拖累大盤
• 阿里巴巴財報揭曉, 營收弱於預期, 印證電商「內需逆風」的窘境: 1)傳統核心電商: 「淘天集團」受制於內需疲弱,
呼應社融與消費數據陰霾, 平台營收成長力道不強; 2) AI相關的「阿里雲」: 具抗週期韌性, 因為企業為了生存反而
迫切導入AI來「降本增效」, 加上雲端系統具備高轉換成本與訂閱制鎖定慣性, 成為財報中少數亮點。
• 美國禁售高階AI晶片, 使阿里採取「外訓內推」的灰色突圍模式。 也就是在東南亞資料中心租用高階算力完成大
模型預訓練, 再將模型用國產晶片進行日常推理服務, 藉此在技術上緊咬國際巨頭。但隨黃仁勳下台、美國恐關閉

第三地算力通道, 恐讓大模型研發進度延遲, 股價單日重挫近7%。展望後市, 在財報不如預期與算力受阻的雙重夾
擊下, 阿里股價目前仍是弱勢格局。此外, 大規模財政刺激暫時無望, 長期均線空頭排列下, A股大盤目前也尚未逆
轉弱勢格局。

Hyperscalers 發行(右軸)
USD HY發行(左軸)
市場對信用審視趨於嚴謹, CCC利差持續走擴 AI融資需求仍推升信用債供給
註: 信用利差BB=182bps、B=311bps、CCC=1083bps。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/11/16。
註: 1)採總發行(Gross Issuance); 截至2026/11/5, hyperscalers已發行2960億美元。
2)預估IG/HY總發行: 2026年2.3兆美元/3400億美元; 2027年2.4兆美元/3750億美元。
資料來源: 高盛預估, 截至2026/11。
• 隨利率中樞上移及融資成本攀升, 低評級企業再融資壓力明顯增加, CCC利差持續高於1000bps, YTD擴張幅度明

顯高於BB及B級。然而, 目前整體HY利差約320bps,仍明顯低於歷次衰退前夕常見的500~700bps區間。反映市場
對利率水準升高的重新定價, 而非出現對硬著陸的系統性擔憂。
• 未來供給壓力仍存, 所幸增幅有望放緩: Hyperscalers發債規模持續增加, 預估2027年發行量將升破4000億美元,
持續形成信用供給壓力; 然而, 隨資料中心建設成長高峰逐步趨緩, 2028年AI資本支出增速有望降溫, 亦有助緩和市
場對現金流的疑慮。在市場已提前反映供給壓力下, 且未來有望增速趨緩前提下, 預期信用利差將維持高檔震盪,
而非持續大幅走擴。配置上, 建議優先布局基本面穩健之IG以及HY中的BB發行人, 並審慎看待CCC券種。
信用債｜CCC信用利差大幅走擴; 未來供給壓力仍存, 惟增幅有望逐步趨緩

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期，維持政策利率不變。決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂，也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對 12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在「通膨高企」與「經濟強韌」雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息，為歐元

提供部分支撐，但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國，在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下，歐元反彈力道相對有限。目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善，但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對 AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪；周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變，美元指數自 102
上方回落，加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐，境外人民幣突破 6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔，美中利差維持在相對高位，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號，USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息，升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、歐元區第三季 GDP
年增 1.3%，高於市場預期的 1.1%，顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場。此外，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%，優於市場預期的 3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破，顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1050-1.1270

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.27-115.10.30 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 2.03% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.845%~1.846%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，時序邁入月底，銀行間已大致調整好積數，且央行未明顯
增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本週台債市場交投依舊清淡，10 年期指標券 A15109R 等殖系統成交在 2.03%，
5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。 
美債利率自波段高點回落，且央行總裁楊金龍本週立法院發言維持近來對通
膨容忍度變寬之態度，惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除，買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週 30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

Wiwynn (6669.TW): ASIC AI servers and General servers to drive growth 
ahead; 3Q26 and Oct in line; Buy
13 November 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
Wiwynn Oct revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$159bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI 
servers (air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we 
model sequential revenue increase in Nov and Dec, considering liquid-cooled ASIC AI 
servers of its major customers, and as we expect GPU AI server racks ramp up in 
4Q26E. We expect 4Q26 revenues growth to remain strong, up to +15% QoQ/ 71% 
YoY, accelerating further to +38% QoQ in 1Q27, driven by AI servers’ new models 
ramp up, AI servers’ expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU 
expansion, and less impact from the general server Buy & Sell to Consignment shift. 
Maintain Buy with TP unchanged at NT$4,000 (19.2x 2027E target PE vs. +37% EPS 
YoY in 2027-28E).

3Q26 results in line: Revenues is pre-announced; GM declined to 8.9%, in line with 
our estimates, while better than Bloomberg consensus of 8.4%, leading GP to +51% 
QoQ, in line with our estimates, or 13% ahead of Bloomberg consensus. The lower 
GM reflects AI servers ramp up, along with less NRE as new models entering mass

production. Meanwhile, opex ratio was down to 1.5%, largely in line with our /

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and Oct revenues, and change our net 
income by 1% / +6% / +0% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues and opex 
ratio: (1) we raise 2026-27E revenues by 2% / 2%, reflecting the ramp up of new AI 
server products, and the company’s potential of new ASIC AI servers customers 
expansion. (2) We cut 2026-27E opex ratio by 0.1 ppts/ 0.2 ppts, reflecting improved 
operation efficiency as the company expands its AI servers. We keep our 2028E 
estimates largely unchanged.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 19.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q26 earnings beat on margins: Better GM of 32.5% (+1.8ppt QoQ) and well-
controlled opex of NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) led to an operating profit of NT
$5,720mn (OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/cons. at NT$5,390mn (OPM
18.9%)/NT$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%).
Power interconnects playing an increasingly important role: We estimate that
power was 65-70% of its HPC revenue in 3Q26 vs. ~50% in the past two years,
which should bring in tailwinds for its blended GM, as power interconnects in

general carry higher margins than data ones. Looking forward, we expect the
increasing power consumption in AI clusters to continue drive the growth from both
volume and spec upgrades in this space. Moreover, future power delivery
architecture could also add incremental opportunities for Bizlink.
Data interconnects diversify into optical: While the market has been concerned
about its data interconnect business recently owing to the focus on the copper

camp, we’d point out that the revenue mix from data interconnects decreased to 30-
35% of HPC in 3Q26 (or 15% of total revenue), per our estimates. Looking ahead, it
is diversifying into the optical space, including micro VCSEL based ALCs along with
its US partner, shuffle boxes/FAU alignment process for CPO and fiber patch cords.
Interplex contribution bigger than expected: Bizlink started to consolidate
Interplex from late Sep and it contributed US$67mn of revenue in Oct, or 18% of

Bizlink’s US$369mn (NT$11,846mn, +27% QoQ/+80% YoY). This is higher than the
market expectation of high single digit %. Our checks indicate that its margins are
above Bizlink’s corporate average, so the earnings might be over 20%, we estimate.
We believe this could make its offerings more comprehensive in the AI cluster and
Interplex could expand its business faster with the financial strength of Bizlink.
Stay OW: We think the growth outlook looks solid and is further strengthened by

the consolidation of Interplex. With the valuation of 11x 2027 P/E approaching the
trough valuation of 8x P/E post the recent pullback, we think risk reward looks
attractive. Stay OW with lower PT of NT$2,500 (18x 2027 P/E).

Results Summary
3Q26 earnings beat on margins
Off revenue of NT$27,875mn (+20% QoQ/+51% YoY), GM expanded by 1.8ppt to 32.5%,
vs. MSe/consensus at 32.0%/31.8%, on a more favorable revenue mix. Opex was also well
controlled at NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) vs. MSe/consensus at NT$3,728mn (opex
ratio 13.1%)/NT$3,615mn (opex taio 13.1%), leading to an operating profit of NT$5,720mn
(OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$5,390mn (OPM 18.9%)/NT

$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%). With the non-op loss of NT$210mn, net profit came in at NT
$4,293mn (EPS NT$22.01), 7%/13% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$3,996mn (EPS NT
$20.67)/NT$3,789mn (EPS NT$19.27).

### Page 6

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
Our new price target (base case scenario value) of  NT$2,500 (NT$3,665 previously) is
derived from 18x 2027e P/E (30x previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation
methodology because we believe it appropriately captures Bizlink's earnings growth
potential. We believe our target P/E multiple of 18x is justified by an earnings CAGR of
55%   over 2025-28, which implies a PEG of  0.3, lower than the average of 0.7 in 2019-24,

when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 13x for a 19% earnings CAGR. We use a
lower target multiple to factor in more conservative sentiment towards AI stocks.
Bull case scenario value – NT$4,100 (vs. prior 4,800): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (vs.
prior 40x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes stronger AEC/ALC
demand owing to broader adoption within AI servers along with spec upgrades.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (vs. prior 1,760): We apply a 8x P/E multiple (vs.

prior 15x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes lukewarm data center
demand amid softening IT capex budgets and pricing pressure for low-barrier products in
other business segments.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
TSMC  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
AI demand and key strategy: TSMC remains confident in the AI mega trend.
Technology roadmap and capex capacity planning are the most critical decisions for
company's long-term development.
US power constraint: Regarding the US power infrastructure shortage concerns,
TSMC has its own internal assessment process and disciplines, and combining with
customers' feedback, there is no change in the demand forecast.

Capacity and US expansion: TSMC is still aiming to more than double its 2nm
capacity by next year, given strong demand from AI and smartphone. The company
also continues to evaluate the next US fab site, and there was a discussion with
Tesla for TeraFab cooperation in parallel.
GPU and ASIC customers: TSMC will continue support major GPU customers, as it
does with other customers' needs. Both GPU and ASIC are TSMC's customers, and
TSMC have been balancing customers' needs.

Pricing and margin: TSMC's pricing reflects its value and cost inflation. Although
semiconductor and foundry remain a cyclical industry, TSMC hopes its margin will
be consistent and sustainable through the cycle.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
TSMC (2330.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-
free rate of 2.0% and risk premium of 6.0%), an intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a
terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
Risks to Upside
n TSMC charging large customers more to keep its gross margin above 56% in the long
term
n AI semi demand growing more significantly than expected while TSMC maintains high

market share in leading-edge foundry business
n Outsourcing from Intel CPU increasing over 2025-27
Risks to Downside
n Inventory correction in 2026
n Weakening demand for leading edge technologies
n Costs of overseas fabs growing significantly

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

貿聯 3Q26 財 報 優 於 預 期 ， 營 業 利 益 率 創 歷 史 新 高 ， 加 上 
Interplex 併表效益優於原先預估，為後續營運增添新亮點。展
望未來，研究部持續看好貿聯中長期成長潛力，主要考量：(1) 隨 
AI 機櫃功耗持續提升，電力傳輸規格朝高電流、高壓化發展，帶
動 Power Whip、Busbar 等產品升級，單櫃電源產值可望倍增；
(2) 高速互連朝 1.6T 以上演進，AEC 與高階光纖業務接續放
量，推升資料傳輸產值；(3) Interplex 併購補足精密機構能力，
結合既有電力及高速互連布局，貿聯可望由零組件供應商逐步延
伸至機櫃次系統整合，擴大單一客戶供應範圍及長期成長商機。
研究部將 Interplex 納入預估，上修 2026、2027 年稀釋後 EPS 
至 73.49 元（+67.4% YoY，原估 68.77 元）及 145.24 元
（+103.2% YoY，原估 117.82 元）。惟考量近期 AI 資料中心建
置不確定性升高，可能使新平台導入與機櫃出貨時程遞延，壓抑
評價表現，研究部將本益比由 25 倍下修至 20 倍，以 2027 年 
EPS 為評價基礎，目標價 2,950 元，投資評等由「買進」調升至
「強力買進」。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 營業利益率創歷史新高，財報優於預期 
貿聯 3Q26 營收 278.75 億元（+19.7% QoQ、+51.4% YoY），符
合預期。其中 HPC 營收成長強勁（+30% QoQ、+61% YoY），營收
占比由 2Q26 的 42% 提升至 46%。隨 AEC 拉貨恢復正常，上半
年產線未達經濟規模的不利影響逐步消除，帶動整體毛利率回升
至 32.53%（+1.80ppt QoQ、−0.72ppt YoY）；加上營收規模擴
大，營業費用率降至 11.75%（−1.76ppt QoQ、−1.97ppt YoY），
推升營業利益率至 20.78%（+3.56ppt QoQ、+1.24ppt YoY），創
歷史新高。營業利益達 57.92 億元（+44.5% QoQ、+61.0% YoY），
優於原估的 52.50 億元。業外因匯兌損失及利息費用合計損失 
2.30 億元，稅後淨利達 42.90 億元（+43.9% QoQ、+63.2% YoY），
優於原估的 39.78 億元，稀釋後 EPS 為 20.34 元，優於原估
的 19.45 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. Interplex 併表貢獻超預期，上修 4Q26 營收與獲利 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.46 億元（+26.9% MoM、+80.0% YoY），創
歷史新高，主要受惠 Interplex 於 9 月底完成交割，10 月首
度完整併表即貢獻營收 6,700 萬美元，約為併購公告時近 12 個
月平均月營收的一倍。Interplex 為 NVIDIA NVLink Switch 機
殼主要供應商，受惠 AI 機櫃需求強勁，併表貢獻高於原先預估。
考量併購效益優於預期，加上既有業務持續成長，研究部上修 
4Q26 營收至 349.76 億元（+25.5% QoQ、+76.8% YoY，原估 
302.69 億元）。預估毛利率為 32.49%（−0.04ppt QoQ、+0.56ppt 
YoY），大致持平前季；隨營收規模擴大及 Interplex 較低費用
率貢獻，營業費用率降至 10.85%，推升營業利益率至 21.64%
（+0.86ppt QoQ、+3.27ppt YoY，原估 20.0%）。預估 4Q26 稅
後淨利達 55.28 億元（+28.9% QoQ、+101.8% YoY），稀釋後 EPS 
由原估 22.38 元上修至 26.21 元（ +28.9% QoQ、+101.8% YoY）。

3. AI 機櫃供電架構升級，電源單櫃產值與市占率同步提升 
隨 AI 伺服器運算密度持續提高，機櫃功耗由 GB200 世代約 
120kW，朝 Vera Rubin 世代 200kW 以上發展，電力傳輸規格同
步升級。在既有低壓直流架構下，功耗提升帶動電流需求增加，
Power Whip 與 Busbar 須升級至更高載流規格，推升單櫃電源
產值；隨機櫃功率持續攀升，傳統 54VDC 架構面臨銅材用量、配
電損耗及機櫃空間限制，NVIDIA 已推動 800VDC 高壓直流架構，
透過提高配電電壓、減少電力轉換環節，支援未來 MW 級 AI 機
櫃需求。貿聯除受惠電源產品規格升級，於 Vera Rubin 世代的 
Busbar 市占率亦可望明顯提升，推動單櫃電源產值較 GB 世代
倍增。考量新平台相關出貨預計於 1H27 起逐季放量，研究部預
估 2027 年電源營收成長約 114% YoY；中長期隨 800VDC 架構
逐步導入，既有 Power Whip、Busbar 及高電流連接技術可望延
伸至新一代配電系統，持續擴大供應商機。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. 高速互連升級至 1.6T，AEC 與高階光纖雙軌成長 
隨 AI 資料中心傳輸頻寬由 800G 朝 1.6T 以上升級，高速互連
對訊號完整性、傳輸距離及功耗的要求持續提高。短距離傳輸仍以
銅纜具成本優勢，較長距離則由光纖滿足頻寬與訊號傳輸需求，預
期 AEC 與光互連將持續並存。AEC 方面，1.6T 新規格因材料成本
及組裝難度提高，產品單價與毛利率可望優於前代，預計於 4Q26 
至 1Q27 逐步導入量產，研究部預估 2027 年 AEC 營收成長約 
41% YoY，主要受惠高階產品占比提升。光纖方面，新富生主要供
應 AI 資料中心機櫃間傳輸所需的 MPO、MT 高密度光纖跳線及連
接器組件，受惠高速光纖互連需求成長，月營收較 2026 年初成長
逾一倍，獲利同步改善。中長期來看，貿聯亦積極布局 Optical 
Shuffle、CPO 交換器方案及 Micro-VCSEL 光互連技術，逐步由傳
統線纜組裝延伸至高階光學組件，為後續成長開拓更多商機。

圖一、貿聯-KY 1Q25～4Q27F HPC 營收結構

資料來源：統一投顧預估

5. 半導體設備需求強勁，非 HPC 業務維持穩健 
貿聯 3Q26 半導體設備營收成長 11% QoQ、70% YoY，為非 HPC 業
務主要成長動能。隨先進製程及半導體設備投資持續推進，客戶需
求由傳統線束延伸至設備模組、精密組裝及次系統整合，帶動單機
供應產值提升。貿聯已深入薄膜沉積與蝕刻設備供應鏈，並逐步拓
展至微影及檢測設備，擴大產品應用範圍。其他業務方面，工廠自
動化、醫療、能源及車用等終端需求維持穩健，提供多元營收來源。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

6. 上修 2026～2028 年獲利預估，營運槓桿與併購效益持續擴
大 
研究部將 Interplex 併表貢獻納入預估，上修貿聯 2026 年營收
至 1,069.96 億元（+50.2% YoY，原估 1,021.18 億元），毛利率
估 31.4%，稀釋後 EPS 73.49 元（+67.4% YoY，原估 68.77 元）。
展望 2027 年，受惠 AI 電源及高速互連業務持續成長，加上 
Interplex 全年併表，預估營收達 1,710.84 億元（+59.9% YoY，
原估 1,517.46 億元）。隨高毛利 HPC 業務占比提升，毛利率可
望升至 34.3%（原估 34.0%）；加上營收規模擴大及 Interplex 
較低費用率貢獻，營業費用率降至 10.2%（原估 13.0%），推升營
業利益率至 24.1%（原估 21.0%），稀釋後 EPS 上修至 145.24 
元（+103.2% YoY，原估 117.82 元）。展望 2028 年，隨 AI 機
櫃供電架構朝 800VDC 演進、高速互連規格持續升級，加上 
Interplex 受惠機櫃需求成長及精密機構件供應範圍擴大，預期將
共同帶動營運成長。研究部預估營收達 2,136.69 億元（+24.9% 
YoY），稀釋後 EPS 196.20 元（+35.1% YoY，原估 171.03 元）。

圖二、貿聯-KY 2025～2028F 各應用別營收占比

資料來源：公司資料及統一投顧預估

7. 中長期成長趨勢明確，投資評等調升至「強力買進」 
研究部持續看好貿聯中長期成長潛力，隨 AI 機櫃電力與高速互
連規格持續升級，帶動單櫃供應產值提升，加上 Interplex 補足
精密機構能力，逐步擴大機櫃次系統供應範圍，有利深化客戶合作
關係並提升市占率。惟考量近期 AI 資料中心建置不確定性升高，
可能使新平台導入及機櫃出貨時程遞延，壓抑市場評價，研究部將
本益比由 25 倍下修至 20 倍。以 2027 年稀釋後 EPS 145.24 
元為評價基礎，目標價 2,950 元，投資評等由「買進」調升至「強
力買進」。

強力買進    預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上

股價相對大盤走勢

達興材料(5234 TT) Trading Buy

半導體材料持續推升年營收成長，評等為 Trading Buy。  
投資建議：雖然台灣面板廠關閉部份產線將影響顯示器材料出
貨表現，不過，預估半導體材料出貨規模將因台灣晶圓代工廠
提高在地供應比重而持續擴大，2027 年營收展望優於 2026 年，
故群益對達興材的投資建議為 Trading Buy。  
3Q26 稅後淨利 2.35 億元，QoQ+2.67%：由於半導體材料出貨
規模擴大，3Q26 營收 13.82 億元，QoQ+7.82%。雖然受到部份
顯示器材料來料異常影響而提列存貨跌價損失，毛利率降低至
41.25%，但在營收規模擴大下，營業利益增加至 2.74 億元，
QoQ+6.60%，稅後淨利 2.35 億元，QoQ+2.67%。  
2027 年半導體材料營收展望佳：達興材 2027 年的營收動能仍
來自半導體材料，達興材除自行開發產品外，亦與國際材料大
廠策略合作，1H26 半導體材料已量產的產品為 13 項，帶動
1Q~3Q26 半導體材料及關鍵原材料營收增加至 9.73 億元，
YoY+52.03%。其中，先進製程重要產品包括自行開發的乾蝕刻
後清洗液，以及 2 項 2 奈米製程材料(1 項為自行開發，1 項為
與國際材料大廠策略合作開發)；成熟製程重要產品為與國際材
料大廠合作開發的光阻用高純度高分子；至於先進封裝重要產
品則為自行開發的雷射離型層、高選擇比銅蝕刻液。由於半導
體材料 2H26 量產產品再新增 5 項，且達興材指出將自 2027 年
開始貢獻營收，搭配中港 2 廠新產能開出，預估半導體材料營
收 YoY+38.25%。  
再就長線出貨展望而言，由於台灣晶圓代工大廠預計在 2030 年
以前將在地採購比重提高至 68%，此一趨勢有利於達興材新產
品打入相關供應鏈。目前達興材半導體材料已送樣至客戶處認
證的產品共 14 項，並另有 8 項產品處於開發階段，均為 2028
年至 2029 年潛在營收動能，達興材並規劃在 2030 年以前將半
導體材料的營收比重由 1Q~3Q26 的 23%提高至 30%。

### Page 2

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 17 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露： 
就溫室氣體排放而言，達興材相關管理策略主要有三，(1)組織面碳管理，每
年依據 ISO14064-1 標準執行溫室氣體盤查與查證，並依循國際標準逐步增
加盤查類別，提高碳資訊揭露透明度，作為推動節能減碳相關工作之基礎；
(2)產品面碳管理，依據 ISO14067 標準執行主要產品碳盤查，掌握產品單位
能耗及碳排放量，逐步建置內部產品碳足跡資料庫；(3)系統面碳管理，建置
內部碳管理平台，將溫室氣體相關電子表單進行系統化管理，以做為定期檢
討營運碳排的管理手段。 
再就廢棄物管理政策或減量目標而言，達興材積極推動廢棄物回收及資源
化，透過容器回收、溶劑回收以及能源回收來達到減量，相關目標主要有二，
(1)廢溶劑回收率(含能源回收)目標為 90%； (2)洗釜溶劑重複利用率目標為
80%。  
雖半導體材料出貨規模擴大，但顯示器來料問題影響毛利率表現，3Q26 稅
後淨利 2.35 億元，QoQ+2.67%：  
達興材為顯示器材料廠，為友達(2409 TT)、長興(1717 TT)合資設置，產品
包括彩色濾光片(color filter)感光間隙材料(全球市佔率第 1)、液晶 (cell)配向
膜、array 銅蝕刻液(主打高階產品市場，在 8K 面板維持領先地位)等，並配
合友達集團跨足巨量轉移暫時接著膠、白色光阻等 Micro LED 面板材料領
域，近幾年合計營收比重均超過 80%。  
此外，達興材亦進軍半導體材料(晶圓級/面板級先進封裝、先進/成熟製程材
料 )、綠能(電動車電池、高階工具機電池材料)市場，並以半導體材料出貨潛
力較佳。首先，在先進封裝材料的部份，產品除包括有機離型層、光阻剝離
液、高選擇比銅蝕刻液等間接材外，亦佈局高解析感光介電材、低溫低介電
感光介電材、耐高溫接著膠等直接材(永久材)；其次，在晶圓製程材料的部
份，產品則包括特用高純度溶劑(成熟製程，供維護、清洗高階管線之用)、
光阻剝離液(成熟製程)、高選擇比銅蝕刻液(先進製程)、高純度高分子(成熟
製程)、底部抗反射層剝離液(成熟製程)等。 
達興材擁有中科廠、中港 1 廠、2 廠，達興材賴以起家的面板材料生產機台
係設置於中科廠、中港 1 廠，至於近期出貨規模持續擴大的半導體材料除少
數機台設置於中科廠、中港 1 廠外，後續新機台則均規畫設置於中港 2 廠
(半導體材料所需之無塵室等級通常高於面板材料)，該廠為 4 層樓建築，目
前 1 樓設置 10 條生產線(均已量產)， 2 樓則規畫於 2027 年以前再陸續建置
10 條生產線，此外，達興材尚規劃配合客戶需求於高雄橋頭設廠，預計 2027
年動工、4Q28 完工，待完工後可就近供貨(最快 2029 年開始貢獻營收)。  
3Q26 營收 13.82 億元，QoQ+7.82%，動能來自半導體材料及關鍵原材料，
相關營收 4.02 億元，QoQ+25.87%，雖然受到部份顯示器材料來料異常影響
而提列存貨跌價損失(影響毛利率 2~2.5%)，毛利率降低至 41.25%(2Q26 為
42.91%)，但在營收規模擴大下，營業利益增加至 2.74 億元，QoQ+6.60%，
稅後淨利 2.35 億元，QoQ+2.67%。  
受惠於新產品、新產能效應，2027 年半導體材料營收展望佳： 
達興材 2027 年的營收動能仍來自半導體材料，達興材除自行開發產品外，

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 17 日

3 群益投顧 
亦與國際材料大廠策略合作，1H26 半導體材料已量產的產品為 13 項，帶動
1Q~3Q26 半導體材料及關鍵原材料營收增加至 9.73 億元，YoY+52.03%。其
中，先進製程重要產品包括自行開發的乾蝕刻後清洗液，以及 2 項 2 奈米製
程材料(1 項為自行開發，1 項為與國際材料大廠策略合作開發)；成熟製程
重要產品為與國際材料大廠合作開發的光阻用高純度高分子；至於先進封
裝重要產品則為自行開發的雷射離型層、高選擇比銅蝕刻液。由於半導體材
料 2H26 量產產品再新增 5 項，且達興材指出將自 2027 年開始貢獻營收，
搭配中港 2 廠新產能開出，預估半導體材料營收 YoY+38.25%。  
再就長線出貨展望而言，由於台灣晶圓代工大廠預計在 2030 年以前將在地
採購比重提高至 68%，此一趨勢有利於達興材新產品打入相關供應鏈。目前
達興材半導體材料已送樣至客戶處認證的產品共 14 項，並另有 8 項產品處
於開發階段，均為 2028 年至 2029 年潛在營收動能，達興材並規劃在 2030
年以前將半導體材料的營收比重由 1Q~3Q26 的 23%提高至 30%。  
此外，在顯示器材料部份，雖然台灣面板廠關閉部份產線的影響程度將自
2H27 開始擴大，不過，達興材已積極拓展中國市場，預估 2027 年顯示器材
料營收仍得以維持與 2026 年接近的水準。 
考量半導體材料推升 2027 年營收持續成長，群益對達興材的投資建議為
Trading Buy：  
雖然台灣面板廠關閉部份產線將影響顯示器材料出貨表現，不過，預估半導
體材料出貨規模將因台灣晶圓代工廠提高在地供應比重而持續擴大，2027
年營收展望優於 2026 年，故群益對達興材的投資建議為 Trading Buy。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

新代(7750)：中國高階控制器需求持續強勁 
建議：評等買進

一、新代為台灣精密設備控制器
大廠 
 新代成立於 1995 年，為專注於精密設備控制器之大廠，以自有品牌 SYNTEC
行銷全球。主營產品涵蓋 CNC 數控系統、伺服產品與產業專機；集團亦積極佈
局新成長動能，旗下「聯達智能」與「正鉑雷射」分別專攻機器人/雲端智造
（IoT/MES）及工業減碳製程設備。公司具備兩大核心優勢：其一為精準的市場
定位，產品品質媲美德日大廠（如 Siemens、Fanuc），同時具備極佳的性價比，
成功填補高階進口與中低階國產間的市場缺口；其二為軟硬整合與在地服務，透
過橫跨歐美、亞太及中國 24 個分公司的全球據點，提供即時服務以協助機械廠
進行差異化升級。目前新代服務逾 2,000 家客戶，單一客戶營收占比均低於 5%，
客戶結構極具分散性與營運韌性，築起穩固的長期競爭護城河。

二、產品組合優化抵銷原物料成
本上漲，3Q26 毛利維持約 44%
水準 
 受惠中國工具機及金屬加工產業需求強勁、終端客戶持續升級高階控制器，以及
競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限帶動轉單，新代 3Q26 營收達 78.05 億元，QoQ-
5.5%、YoY+157.7%，雖受傳統淡季影響較上季下滑，但仍創歷史單季次高。獲
利方面， 儘管記憶體價格上漲，受惠高階控制器銷售比重提升及產品組合優化，
3Q26 毛利率達 44.44%，季增 0.35 個百分點、年減 0.04 個百分點，維持公司
長期指引 45%±2%區間； 隨營收規模擴大及費用率改善，3Q26 營業利益達
21.06 億元，QoQ-11.9%、YoY+398.2%，營業利益率達 26.98%，年增 13.02
個百分點；稅後淨利達 16.80 億元，QoQ-11.4%、YoY+360.2%，單季 EPS 達
24.06 元，創歷史單季次高。1-3Q26 稅後淨利達 46.50 億元，YoY+221.7%，
EPS 達 66.59 元，反映高階產品需求成長、轉單效益及營運規模擴張持續挹注
獲利。

三、中國高階控制器滲透率持續
提升，海外布局與產能擴張挹注
2027 年成長動能 
 2026 年中國工具機產業維持成長動能，1–8 月金屬切削相關產業成長約 18–
19%，受惠製造業升級、五軸加工及車銑複合機等高階設備需求增加，帶動控制
器與伺服系統銷售。新代憑藉高階產品性能、較國際競品約 20%的價格優勢，
以及中國逾 2,000 家活躍客戶與在地直銷服務網絡，持續擴大市占率，2026 年
控制器出貨量預估 YoY+70–80%，並受惠日系競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限，
取得大型工具機客戶轉單。儘管部分日系廠商供貨逐步恢復，公司透過提前備貨
及與記憶體原廠簽訂供應協議，預期關鍵零組件供應可維持至 2027 年下半年，
搭配產品組合優化及客戶黏著度提升，有望延續競爭優勢。海外布局方面，印度
市場受惠製造業在地化政策及工具機產業升級需求，帶動高階控制器國產替代
與供應商多元化商機。公司已與印度前十大工具機廠建立合作接洽，並取得兩筆
單筆超過 100 套的訂單，預期 2026 年印度營收 YoY+100%，惟目前營收貢獻

仍低；中長期目標五年內印度市占率提升至 10%，並持續擴大東南亞市場布局。
產能方面，蘇州二廠預計於 2027 年農曆年前後完成搬遷，馬來西亞新廠預計
1Q27 完工，隨新產能陸續投入，預計 1H27 整體產能將擴充至目前約 2.6 倍，
另有機械手臂產能增加，為後續營收成長提供支撐。展望 2027 年，管理層預期
中國工具機產業成長約 10–15%，公司目標營收成長 25–30%，並持續推動新代
控制器、聯達機械手臂及正鉑智慧工廠整合方案，搭配海外市場拓展及 AI 技術
導入，帶動營收與獲利持續成長。

四、評等買進理由  綜合前述，展望 2027 年，新代受惠中國高階工具機需求成長、日系競爭對手轉
單效益延續，以及控制器市占率持續提升，營運成長動能可望延續。隨蘇州二廠
及馬來西亞新廠陸續投產，預計自 2Q27 起新增產能逐步開出，整體控制器產能
有望擴充至目前約 2.5 倍，搭配印度大型工具機客戶導入加速，以及多合一高階
控制器與智慧自動化產品持續推出，有助推升產品 ASP 及獲利能力，預估 2026
年稅 EPS 為 91.49，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 118.49。至於評價面部分，過往
3 年 PER 平均落在 25x-35x，目前約 16x，處歷史區間下緣，中國市占率提升、
印度市場拓展、新產能貢獻及智慧製造業務成長等中長期動能，認為目前評價具
吸引力，故給予買進評等，目標價 2300 元(25x2026EPS)。

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) 
3Q26 Review: 기대치 하회. 이제는 용기를 낼 시점

주가 추이

3분기 영업이익은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일회성 비용 등의 영향으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복과 중장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸류에이션에 주목합니다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking
the cash rate after the November increase. Since then, our
conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we
will publish the November Market Outlook with revised
forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing

market have all been revised down in 2027.
Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for
calendar 2027 more decisively inside the RBA’s 2–3% target
range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment
rate forecast has been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on
this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash rate from
here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.

A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The
housing market has undershot our forecasts over the past
couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been
coming in under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan
inquiries have taken another leg down in October after the
September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior
months, with the additional impact of the November rate hike
still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer.

Consumer income and spending have so far been quite
resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest
rates. But our Westpac Card Tracker is starting to show that
discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite
weak. We would not over-emphasise this point, but it does
imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB)

meeting, business survey data has shown weaker conditions
and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show
further evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-
labour cost shock that is not being fully passed through to
consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil
and fuel prices are already materially lower than the track
assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-
fourth lower than on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts

were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of
fuel costs to other prices, as well as easing consumer inflation
perceptions and expectations.
An RBA peak of greater conviction
Forecast changes
• A full update to our forecasts has been published in our
November Market Outlook.
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
In the media conference following the November meeting,
Governor Bullock flagged that it was a good time to “take

stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the
market read this as a signal that the MPB would pause at the
December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to
downside risks than the internal members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is
looking even less likely given signs in the data and surveys.
The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and

senior officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a
downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though,
a rate hike in 2027 would require either a material new
inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would
constitute a policy error on the part of the RBA (something
that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the talents
and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with

the dissenting voters in the MPB that the downside risks need
more attention.
Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to
February, we think there will be more evidence that the
domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation
outlook with it.
“A deeper housing downturn and early signs of
cracks in the consumer sector point to an even
softer 2027."

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
the labour market while showing significant concern over
the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the
passthrough of energy and technology input inflation. His
conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the monthly

rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward
supporting an increase in our policy rate of 25 basis points
at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI
inflation data out later today are similar to the past two
monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent
or even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also
kept the focus on inflation risks and stated or implied the FOMC

needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s
updates, pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January
meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by September. We believe
this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core
inflation ex shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-
month annualised basis and the labour market is balanced; but
it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate,

US households are under pressure from the cost of living, and
their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing
costs. Highlighting this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in
October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and
2010. Our November Market Outlook provide a full update on
our expectations for the US and the world.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index

fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest reading since
late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of
severe economic stress. Renewed cost-of-living and interest
rate pressures were front and centre following another lift
in inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the
RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage holders are feeling
the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group
falling to a record low. Consumers have become noticeably

more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the
relevant sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its
long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business
survey showed confidence slipped further to –15 in October as
conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic.
Of concern for the outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also
the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow
materially faster than selling prices, further compressing
already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter
financial conditions can jolt activity. The value of new dwelling
finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly
decline in the series’ history, taking approvals 25% lower year-
to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined

with the housing tax changes announced in May. Owner-
occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home
buyers proving particularly sensitive to the deterioration in
affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum
across the domestic economy, consistent with Westpac’s
expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside
activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our

expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the consensus
view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the
result, but core inflation was also marginally stronger than
forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the
past four months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look
to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost inflation through.
Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace,
gaining 0.3% in October.

The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated
by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor Waller prior to the
release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Post election, where are we left?
This week markets and the economy needed to digest
the General Election results, as well as data on how the
economy is tracking through the final months of the year. A
centre-right coalition looks more likely to ultimately prevail,

although it may be a while before we know for sure and
longer again before a stable government emerges. Watch
this space as paths to victory for the centre-left remain.
Economic data are consistent with a still-strong external
sector but a weaker domestically-generated growth
impulse. Our upcoming Economic Overview next week will
examine these trends in more depth.
The real star of the show this week was the outcome of the
2026 General Election, which delivered an election night lead

to the centre-right coalition of the National, ACT and NZ First
parties. The preliminary result was a 67-57 seat margin in an
enlarged 131 seat parliament. Hence the centre-right won on
election night with a slender one-seat margin, given that 66
seats are required to form a majority. Financial markets and
the business sector generally cheered this outcome given their
preference for a centre-right government. However, the margins
are tight and there is the potential for the result to switch around

when the final vote count becomes available on November 27.
National leads Labour by narrow margins in two electorates
(Wairarapa and Hamilton East) and could lose those seats
when all is said and done. Also, special votes tend to swing
more to the left than the right historically and hence could
boost the centre-left bloc. The star performer of the election
was the Opportunity Party which debuted in parliament with
7 seats. The centre-left would require their support to get

over the line in all scenarios, whereas the centre-right might or
might not need them depending on how the special votes and
final electorate results play out. There seems a clearer path to
victory for the centre-right – especially if they can come to an
agreement with Opportunity – but clients should be wary of
complacency (which we note may be a risk).
A further complication is that the election left the incumbent
National Party in a much-weakened position as their coalition

partners picked up seats at their expense, and because their
much-reduced share of the party vote (29.2%) meant that
no National Party list MPs made it into parliament. Several
seasoned and senior government ministers needed a list seat
to make it into the new government including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. Even if the final results confirm a centre-right
coalition win, there will be fierce debate within the coalition
on which party staffs key ministerial roles. ACT party leader

David Seymour is making a strong play for Finance Minister,
which Prime Minister Christopher Luxon is fiercely resisting.
There is the potential for tough and protracted coalition
negotiations to play out well into the Christmas period.
Indeed, I spoke on a post-election panel with an ex-National
Party senior minister this week who expressed the view that
it might not be until Waitangi Day (February 6) before a new
government is formed. Should anything like this play out then

the implications for economic and market uncertainty could
be significant. The initial relief rally in the New Zealand dollar
might prove premature in that scenario.
For interest rate markets, the main conclusion drawn is that as
a centre-left government seems a lower probability prospect,
then so also is the likelihood of a return to a ‘dual mandate’
for the RBNZ. Chief Economist Paul Conway made remarks
this week suggesting ongoing concern with inflation risks,

suggesting he is still in the hike camp at this stage. This has
seen markets firm up short-term OCR expectations such that a
25bp OCR increase in December is now seen as high probability
(85-90% currently). Bond markets cheered the prospect of
potentially tighter fiscal policy that could come with a centre-
right government. The spread between the 10-year government
bond and swap spread narrowed 4 basis points over the week
and the 10-year government bond yield differential to the US

fell to -49 basis points – the most negative since 2019.
Economic data showed further evidence of the two-speed
economy that is showing above-trend growth yet at the same
time feels quite unsatisfying to most voters. The low exchange
rate, generally robust global demand and tight supply
conditions in soft commodity markets have been delivering
consistently strong export returns for a couple of years now.
This week we saw another very strong GDT Pulse auction that

propelled whole milk powder futures higher – up some 20-25%
on year ago levels in USD terms. Significant upside risk for our
2026/27 Farmgate Milk Price forecast of $9.75/kgMS now exists,
which we will be delving into more deeply in our Economic
Overview to be released on Wednesday. The manufacturing
sector appears to remain in a solid state, with the manufacturing
PMI slipping a touch in October to 54.9 from 56.3 in September.
Short-term visitor arrivals have resumed their prior strong

uptrend, growing almost 3% for a second consecutive month
in September and almost 10% higher than a year earlier.
While arrivals from key European markets remain well below
year-earlier levels – likely reflecting the disruption to travel
through key Middle Eastern hubs along with higher jet fuel
prices – this has been more than offset by a very sharp lift in
arrivals from China. Indeed, Chinese arrivals in September
were almost two-thirds higher than the same month last year.

Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

### Page 5

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
And in the September quarter, arrivals from China surpassed
their pre-pandemic level for the first time. The number of
Australian citizens visiting New Zealand grew just under 6%yr
in September, but growth momentum from New Zealand’s
number one source market (a 30% share) does appear to have

slowed somewhat in recent months.
Less positive trends were evident in the more domestically
orientated areas of the economy. The most concrete indicator
of the NZ economy – concrete poured – dropped back a
touch in the September quarter, while consumer spending
in fell 0.7% in October. We have concerns on the quality
of the consumer spending data given that bank-sourced
data indicates a rise in consumer spending in October. But
nonetheless it’s probably reasonable to infer that higher fuel

costs in October crimped household budgets.
Housing market data for October were also week. House sales
fell and are down almost 22% y/y, while house prices fell in
October for the fifth consecutive month to be down almost
2% y/y. The weak performance of the housing market is likely
another factor weighing on consumer spending and residential
investment. That’s another topic we will be taking a look at in
next week’s Economic Overview.
Net migration flows for September confirm still modest

population growth. Arrivals of non-New Zealand citizens
continues to drift higher while departures declined a touch.
The net outflow of New Zealanders remains relatively high but
is declining, as signs of firmer employment growth emerge in
New Zealand at the same time as the Australian labour market
shows signs of weakening.

### Page 6

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Employment Change (000s)
Nov 19, Last: –39.3, Westpac f/c: 30
Market f/c: 27.5, Range: 9.9 to 42
Employment fell by 39.3k in September, well below expectations
for a 20k lift. Some of this weakness may reflect an unwind of
a possible residual seasonality stemming from recent changes

to the survey design, which saw August come in stronger.
Under the monthly noise, the labour market is loosening
gradually as jobs growth runs slower than population growth.
While some leading indicators point to near-term stability
– such as job ads – business surveys and detail within the
employment data point to some downside risk.
For October, we have pencilled in a lift in employment of +30k,
which would see employment growth hold steady on a three-

month average basis.
AUS: Nov Westpac Housing Sentiment (index)
Nov 17, Last: 85.3
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index that appears in our
regular Housing Pulse reports will now be published on a
monthly basis a week after Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Sentiment is released. The Index is a composite
based on housing-related sentiment measures from the survey
and has proven to be an extremely useful predictor of turning
points in housing markets, both nationally and across states.

At 85.3, the Oct monthly Index read was holding near historic
lows, well below the long run avg of 100. The Nov update will
capture reactions to the RBA’s latest interest rate rise and the
deepening and broadening housing downturn. The release
will also include results from two new questions added to
the regular survey on consumers’ property buying and selling
intentions over the next twelve months.
AUS: Q3 Wage Price Index (%qtr)
Nov 18, Last: 0.8, Westpac f/c: 1.0

Market f/c: 1.0, Range: 0.9 to 1.1
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 0.8% in Q2, leaving annual
growth at 3.2%. Private sector wages continued to decelerate,
in line with a softer labour market, while public sector wages
provided a bit of an offset.
The Fair Work Commission's (FWC) 2026 Annual Wage Review
handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award
wages. This will have a noticeable impact on the Q3 WPI, as the
wage increases stemming from the review are typically passed

on during the September quarter.
We have pencilled in a rise of 1.0% for Q3, lifting the annual
pace to 3.4%yr. Detail on wages growth by bargaining stream
will be closely watched for evidence of broader diffusion
beyond arrangements directly linked to the FWC's decision.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Unemployment Rate (%)
Nov 19, Last: 4.7, Westpac f/c: 4.7
Market f/c: 4.7, Range: 4.6 to 4.8
Once again, the surprise in employment coincided with a surprise
in labour force participation, which fell 0.2ppts to 66.8% from
its near cycle high of 67.0% in August. This translated to a 26.2k

decline in the size of the labour force, which was less than the
drop in employment. As a result, the unemployment rate ticked
slightly higher in September, from 4.65% to 4.72% (although it
was steady at 4.7% to one decimal place), a new cycle high.
For October, we have the participation rate steady at 66.8%,
which together with the forecast rise in employment, would
see the unemployment rate also hold steady at 4.7%. Early
signs of rising retrenchments are a key signal to watch over the

coming months, especially if new hiring across other areas of
the economy starts to dry up.
NZ: Oct Selected Consumer Prices
Nov 17
Stats NZ’s monthly Selected Prices provides an update on
around half of the full CPI, including many of the more volatile
items. In terms of the big groups, we’re expecting a 0.1%
rise in overall food prices, with fresh produce prices typically
soft at this time of year. We also expect continued softness
in housing rents. However, the key focus will be on transport

costs. Petrol prices are expected to be up 11%. And we’ll be
watching to see if high global fuel prices are contributing to
further increases in airfares after recent large increases. There
is related uncertainty around holiday accommodation costs
(which drive much of the month-to-month volatility in overall
prices).
NZ: Q4 RBNZ Survey of Expectations (%yr)
Nov 17, Inflation 2yrs Ahead, Last: 2.34
With an easing in headline inflation in the September

quarter, we’re likely to see some easing in near-term inflation
expectations (one year ahead). However, longer term inflation
expectations, particularly the closely-watched two years
ahead measure, are likely to remain broadly steady. While
interest rates are moving higher, businesses are continuing to
report pressures on operating costs. In addition, despite their
recent easing, oil prices remain elevated. The only time longer-
term inflation expectations have moved materially below 2.3%

was in the mid-2010s. That followed an extended period when
inflation was running below 2%.

Document 393: Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-20).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

#wages #inflation #labour
Q3 Wage Price Index: award wage boost
By Ryan Wells
Economist, Westpac
16:06 November 18 2026 Share
The minimum wage and award decision lifted wages growth, but unevenly – enterprise
arrangements responded more than individual arrangements, consistent with a softer
labour market. Q3 2026: 1.1%qtr, 3.5%yr.
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 1.1% in Q3 2026, slightly above Westpac’s forecast and the
market consensus for a 1.0% gain. That increased the annual growth rate to 3.5%yr – stronger than

### Page 2

Wages by Bargaining Stream
the past six months, but a similar pace to 2025.
Today’s data also showcased the impact of the 2026 Fair Work Commission’s Annual Wage Review,
which handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award wages. This lifted wages in
aggregate, but the gains were uneven.
A significant share of the increase was driven by health care and social assistance, reflecting pay
rises for state government workers covered by enterprise agreements.

In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3. Awards and enterprise arrangements accounted for 64%
of the lift, while individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share for Q3 since 2019.
Enterprise arrangements, which are often directly linked to award outcomes, were more responsive,
while individual arrangements were less so compared to previous September quarters. That is
consistent with a softer labour market and recent business survey evidence that suggests the rise in

private sector labour costs following the award decision quickly normalised.
Overall, we see the Q3 result as a step up in the level of wages from the award decision rather than
evidence of building underlying wage pressure but we will be watching whether the strength in
enterprise bargaining persists.

### Page 3

Westpac has long believed focusing on wage inflation by wage bargaining stream can help to
understand how wages respond to the economic cycle. Australia’s labour market is split into three
categories of pay setting methods:
Australian wage adjustments, including the Fair Work Commission’s (FWC) minimum wage and award
rate decisions, tend to occur at the start of the new financial year in July. The FWC handed down a
4.75% increase for 2026, larger than the decisions from 2025 (3.50%) and 2024 (3.75%), but lower

than in 2023 (5.75%).
In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3 2026. Of that increase, 0.50ppts were attributed to awards,
which was broadly in line with our expectations. However, both individual and enterprise arrangements
surprised us to the upside, contributing 0.64ppts and 0.65ppts to the quarterly increase in wages
respectively.
National minimum wage and award rates, regulated by the Fair Work Ombudsman,
Enterprise agreements, or
Individual arrangements.

### Page 4

Compared to the same quarter in previous years, each stream's contribution was the strongest in
absolute terms since 2023, largely because the overall lift was bigger than in 2024 and 2025. After
accounting for the size of the increase, enterprise arrangements were responsible for 64% of the lift,
whereas individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share since 2019.
While the larger award wage decision pushed up the overall increase, it did not flow through evenly.

Many enterprise agreements are directly linked to award outcomes, either through explicit reference to
award rates or because the award decision sets a benchmark in bargaining, which helps explain why
this stream was more responsive. By contrast, wages set under individual arrangements can be more
discretionary and depend more on employers' circumstances, so they were less responsive than in
previous years. That may reflect a softer labour market, where weaker demand for workers reduces the

pressure on employers to lift pay.
Wages by Sector
By sector, private wages (excl. bonuses) rose 1.0% in Q3, pulling up the annual pace from 3.1%yr to
3.4%yr. This marks the strongest pace of wages growth in the private sector since 2023, at the time of
the Fair Work Commission’s decision to raise award wages by a much larger 5.75%.
Private sector pay is set through a mix of arrangements, including a significant share of individual
arrangements (as discussed above). Today's result suggests this year's award wage decision may

have had some flow-on effect to the broader private sector, though, as noted, the pass-through was
uneven and appears to have been stronger for enterprise arrangements than for individual
arrangements.
There was also an element of both breadth and scale when it comes to private sector wage gains. Half
of all private sector jobs recorded a wage increase in Q3, the largest share since this series began in
2016; and of those jobs that recorded an increase, average hourly wages rose 4.5%, the strongest

since Q3 2023 (5.8%).
Public wages continue to outpace the private sector, this time with a 1.3% lift in Q3, bringing the annual
growth pace to 3.9%yr, the strongest since Q4 2025. As expected, the bulk of the gain was driven by
pay rises under enterprise agreements at the state government level, particularly for front-line workers.
Additionally, given scheduled rises under existing APS-wide enterprise agreements for Commonwealth
employees had already been delivered in previous quarters, the Commonwealth level contribution to

public sector wages growth during Q3 was modest.
Relative to the private sector, the driver of public sector wage gains looks to be more about scale than
breadth – across the 38% of public sector jobs recording a wage increase during the quarter the
average hourly wage change was 4.0%, slightly under the record high of a 4.3% recorded back in Q4
2023.

### Page 5

Summary
The Q3 result shows that the larger minimum wage and award rate decision boosted wages but the
detail suggests the lift was driven more by workers with stronger bargaining power. Awards and
enterprise arrangements, where pay outcomes are more directly tied to the Fair Work Commission’s
decision or to collective bargaining, whereas individual arrangements, which are more exposed to
labour market conditions, contributed the smallest share since 2019.

That is consistent with a softer labour market – the unemployment rate has risen ½ppt since the start of
the year, and we are expecting it to peak at 5.2% mid next year. The NAB business survey points the
same way, with the increase in labour costs reported by private sector firms following the award
decision quickly normalising. This suggests the Q3 result is better read as a temporary step up in the
level of wages from the award wage decision than as evidence of building underlying wage pressure,

though we will be watching whether the strength in enterprise bargaining persists.

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-星間鏈路ISL為邁向太空AI重要關鍵技術-20261119.pdf

1 
產業更新 – 衛星產業 
星間鏈路 ISL 為邁向太空 AI 重要關鍵技術 
What’s New –  星間鏈路逐步成熟，帶動衛星傳輸效率提升 
隨著低軌衛星產業由早期單純提供地面通訊覆蓋，逐步朝向全球高速寬頻、即時資料傳輸及太
空運算網路發展，星間鏈路（Inter-Satellite Link, ISL）已從衛星系統中的輔助通訊技術，逐
漸演變為支撐大型衛星星座運作的核心基礎設施。透過在衛星之間建立直接通訊鏈路，使資料
得以在軌道上進行多節點轉送，再由適當的衛星回傳至地面，進而降低對密集地面站的依賴，
並提升網路覆蓋範圍、路由彈性及系統備援能力。相較傳統射頻通訊，雷射通訊具備高頻寬、
窄波束及較低跨鏈路干擾等優勢，成為新一代衛星星座的重要發展方向。 
Trends/Outlook – SpaceX 持續提升 ISL 規模 
Starlink 已建立全球規模的太空雷射網狀通訊架構，使衛星接收的資料能夠透過多顆衛星進行
跨區域轉送，降低對地面閘道站的依賴，並有效擴大海洋、極地及偏遠地區的通訊覆蓋能力。
就產品規格而言，目前 Starlink既有衛星 V2 mini採用每顆配置 3組光學雷射終端的架構，單
鏈路最高傳輸速率達 200Gbps，而新一代 V3衛星則進一步增加至 6組雷射終端，單鏈路傳輸
速率提升至 400Gbps，顯示 SpaceX正同步透過增加終端數量及提高傳輸頻寬，擴充整體星座
的資料交換能力。此外，SpaceX 亦逐步將原本供內部使用的雷射通訊技術推向外部商業市場，
推出傳輸速率達 25Gbps的 Mini Laser終端，使第三方衛星得以接入 Starlink網路，提供在軌
資料中繼及高速資料回傳服務。 
Investment Summary 
1. 展望未來，ISL 將成為推動衛星產業由通訊服務邁向太空資訊基礎設施的重要關鍵技術。
預期 SpaceX Starmind中的 AI1運算衛星在獨立衛星 AI節點進行計算後，其結果將透過
ISL雷射互傳形成 AI 叢集，方便將上百上千顆的衛星結果進行聯通，並且及時回傳於地面。
故整體太空 AI資料中心的運行將大幅仰賴 ISL技術的成熟，看好相關衛星通訊元件廠商如
萊德光電(7717 TT)、昇達科(3491 TT)後市發展。 
2. 預期 SpaceX 26/27年新增 2,800/4,000 顆衛星，其中 27年除通訊衛星 V3外，新一代直
連服務衛星亦將起量。SpaceX甫順利完成第 15次星艦試射，將加速 V3衛星部屬，故持
續看好天上衛星 CCL與 PCB商台光電(2383 TT)、華通(2313 TT)後市發展。

光學星間鏈路逐步成熟，帶動衛星傳輸效率提升 
隨著低軌衛星（LEO）產業由早期單純提供地面通訊覆蓋，逐步朝向全球高速寬頻、即時資料傳輸
及太空運算網路發展，星間鏈路（Inter-Satellite Link, ISL）已從衛星系統中的輔助通訊技術，逐
漸演變為支撐大型衛星星座運作的核心基礎設施。過去傳統衛星通訊主要仰賴衛星與地面閘道站之
間建立連線，衛星接收的資料需先回傳至地面，再透過地面骨幹網路進行交換與轉送，因此服務範
圍容易受到地面站部署位置、地理環境及基礎建設覆蓋程度限制。隨著 Starlink等大型低軌衛星星
座加速部署，衛星數量持續增加，全球通訊需求亦逐步延伸至海洋、極地、航空及其他地面網路難
以覆蓋的區域，使傳統高度依賴地面站的傳輸架構逐漸面臨限制，遞訊號路徑如同光透過平面鏡反
射一樣稱為彎管連結，衛星必須同時能被終端設備與地面站接收到，造成偏遠地區、海洋上若無地
面站建置則無法達成衛星通訊服務。 
圖一：衛星間傳輸路徑比較                    
資料來源：前瞻科技管理、國泰證期研究部整理 
尤其隨著光學星間鏈路（Optical ISL）逐漸成熟，透過在衛星之間建立直接通訊鏈路，使資料得以
在軌道上進行多節點轉送，再由適當的衛星回傳至地面，進而降低對密集地面站的依賴，並提升網
路覆蓋範圍、路由彈性及系統備援能力。相較傳統射頻通訊，雷射通訊具備高頻寬、窄波束及較低
跨鏈路干擾等優勢，成為新一代衛星星座的重要發展方向。 
表一：衛星雷射通訊與傳統 RF 通訊對照 
比較項目 衛星雷射通訊（Lasercom） 傳統微波（RF）通訊 
技術原理 利用近紅外光傳輸數位訊號 利用無線電波（如 Ka/Ku 頻段）傳輸 
傳輸頻寬／速率 極高 中低 
頻譜限制 無限制 受限，需嚴格申請頻道，資源擁擠 
安全性 高 低 
固定距離通訊成本 低 高 
設備／架設成本 初期極高（高精密對準系統） 成熟且低（技術標準化、地面設備普及） 
主要缺點 易受氣候影響，對準精度要求極高 頻譜稀缺、延遲較高、易受電子干擾

新世代衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
SpaceX 為目前全球最積極部署 ISL 的衛星營運商之一，透過旗下 Starlink 低軌衛星星座，已將光
學星間通訊技術由早期研發驗證推進至大規模商業應用。SpaceX 最早於 2020 年開始進行衛星間
雷射通訊測試，並成功透過兩顆衛星傳輸大量數據；2021年 1月首次將搭載雷射星間鏈路的 10顆
Starlink衛星部署至極地軌道，隨後於同年 9月正式導入配備 ISL的 Starlink V1.5衛星，逐步將雷
射通訊技術擴展至大規模星座部署。  
隨著衛星數量持續增加，Starlink 已建立全球規模的太空雷射網狀通訊架構，使衛星接收的資料能
夠透過多顆衛星進行跨區域轉送，降低對地面閘道站的依賴，並有效擴大海洋、極地及偏遠地區的
通訊覆蓋能力。就產品規格而言，目前 Starlink既有衛星 V2 mini採用每顆配置 3組光學雷射終端
的架構，單鏈路最高傳輸速率達 200Gbps，而新一代 V3衛星則進一步增加至 6組雷射終端，單鏈
路傳輸速率提升至 400Gbps，顯示 SpaceX 正同步透過增加終端數量及提高傳輸頻寬，擴充整體
星座的資料交換能力。此外，SpaceX 亦逐步將原本供內部使用的雷射通訊技術推向外部商業市場，
推出傳輸速率達 25Gbps的 Mini Laser終端，使第三方衛星得以接入 Starlink網路，提供在軌資料
中繼及高速資料回傳服務。 
圖三：Starlink 雷射通訊系統示意圖 
資料來源：SpaceX、國泰證期研究部整理 
展望未來，隨著衛星直連終端、太空資料中心及軌道運算等應用逐步發展，ISL 的重要性將不再侷
限於改善地面網路覆蓋，而是進一步支援跨衛星資料交換、分散式運算及天地整合網路架構，成為
推動衛星產業由通訊服務邁向太空資訊基礎設施的重要關鍵技術。預期 SpaceX Starmind中的 AI1
運算衛星在獨立衛星 AI節點進行計算後，其結果再透過 ISL雷射互傳形成 AI 叢集，方便將上百上
千顆的衛星結果進行聯通，並且及時回傳於地面，故未來太空 AI資料中心的運行將大幅仰賴 ISL技
術的成熟。

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 5 日

1/4 
インドネシア経済は堅調な成長続くも、その持続性には不透明感が残る 
～７-９月 GDP は加速も、金融市場の混乱長期化、高成長と投資低迷の乖離に懸念～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 2026 年のインドネシア金融市場では、MSCI による株式市場の情報開示への懸念や格下げ観
測を背景に株価が大幅に下落している。さらに、通貨ルピア相場も下落しているほか、長期
金利の高止まりも重なるなど、混乱が続いている。原油高や通貨安によるインフレ圧力が強
まるなか、中銀は景気下支えと通貨安定の両立を模索する動きをみせている。 
○ 金融市場の混乱の影響が懸念されたものの、７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前年同期比＋
5.47％に加速し、資源輸出や個人消費の底堅さが景気を下支えしている。ただし、政府消費
や固定資本投資は弱含んでおり、輸入減少や在庫の積み増しが成長率を押し上げるなど、景
気の数字と実態の間に乖離が生じている可能性には注意が必要である。 
○ プラボウォ政権は 2029 年までに経済成長率を８％に引き上げる目標を掲げるものの、先行
きは金融市場の混乱が、幅広く投資活動の重しとなることが懸念される。経常赤字基調の同
国にとって、成長率目標の実現には安定的な海外資金の流入が不可欠である。その意味では、
今後は市場改革や構造改革を通じて投資家の信認回復を図ることが重要となっている。

【2026 年のインドネシア金融市場は、株式、通貨、債券のすべてに売り圧力がかかる展開】 
2026 年のインドネシア金融市場は混乱に見舞われている。きっかけは、株価指数算出会社の MSCI
が１月末に同国株式市場の情報開示を巡って懸念を表明したことにある。同社は、情報開示の透明
性向上に向けた取り組みが進まなければ、同社が算出する世界株指数などにおける同国株の組み入
れ比率を引き下げるほか、フロンティア市場に格下げする可能性を示唆した。同業の FTSE ラッセ
ルも、定例で実施する予定だった同国株式指数の見直しを延期すると発表した。このため、その後
の主要株価指数（ジャカルタ総合指数）は、格下げに伴うパッシブ運用の投資家を中心とする資金
流出への警戒から、調整の動きを強めた。

### Page 2

2/4 
その後、当局は最低浮動株比率の大幅な引き上げなど、海外投資家の懸念解消に向けた対策を打
ち出し、市場改革を加速させる姿勢を示した。しかし、MSCI は 10 月、同国市場を「スタンドアロ
ーン市場」に格下げする案を示し、市場参加者との協議を開始すると発表した（注１）。 同社はその
理由として、当局による改革方針の発表
後も、市場参加者から協調的な取引行為
への疑念が解消されておらず、実効性の
ある法執行も確認できないとの指摘が
寄せられたことを挙げた。また、フロン
ティア市場への格下げでは、問題の深刻
さが適切に反映されないとの意見も寄
せられた。このため、同社は 2027 年４
月末まで市場参加者から意見を募り、同
年６月末までに最終判断を示す方針を
明らかにした。こうした状況を受けて、
足元の主要株価指数は上値の重い展開が続いており、年初に記録した最高値のおよそ半分の水準で
推移している（図１）。 
足元では、通貨ルピアの対ドル相場も最安値近傍で推移するなど、上値の重い展開が続いている
（図２）。同国ではここ数年、中銀の独立
性への懸念が高まるとともに、プラボウ
ォ政権による拡張的な財政運営への警
戒もルピア相場の重しとなってきた。さ
らに、イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油高
は、補助金関連の歳出増加を招き、財政
運営への懸念を一段と高めている。エネ
ルギーや食料品など生活必需品を中心
とする物価上昇に加え、ルピア安による
輸入物価の押し上げも重なり、足元のイ
ンフレ率は加速するなど、インフレ懸念
が高まっている。こうしたなか、中銀は 10 月の定例会合において、政策金利を据え置く一方、景
気下支えや国内の流動性確保を目的に 11 月から段階的に預金準備率を引き下げるとともに、銀行
からの資金吸収を促すべく預金ファシリティー金利を引き上げるなど、金融緩和と通貨安定の両立
を図る姿勢を示した（注２）。 中銀がこうした対応に動いた背景には、ここ数年の中銀法改正を通じ

注１ 10 月16 日付レポート「インドネシア株に逆風、MSCI が「スタンドアローン」への格下げ検討」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666610.html） 
注２ 10 月21 日付レポート「インドネシア中銀､市場動揺も､政策運営は政府と市場の「板挟み」が続く」

3/4 
て、物価や通貨の安定に加え、経済成長を支援する役割も重視されるようになったことが影響して
いると考えられる。しかし、こうした政策対応を巡る不透明感も、ルピア安圧力が一向に収まらな
いほか、長期金利も高止まりする要因となっている可能性がある。

【金融市場の混乱やインフレの影響が懸念されたが、足元の景気は引き続き堅調な推移が続く】 
こうした金融市場の混乱に加え、イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油高などによるインフレも重なり、
実体経済に下押し圧力がかかることが
懸念された。にもかかわらず、７-９月の
実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前年同期比＋
5.47％と前期（同＋5.29％）から加速す
るなど、足元の景気は引き続き堅調に推
移していることが確認されている（図
３）。当研究所が試算した季節調整値に
基づく前期比年率ベースの成長率も加
速して２四半期ぶりの高い伸びとなる
など、足元の景気は持ち直しの動きを強
めている様子がうかがえる。 
原油高を受けた代替需要の高まりを反映して石炭価格も高止まりしており、同国政府は国内供給
を優先する観点から、石炭や天然ガスの輸出を事実上制限する動きを強めたため、輸出への悪影響
が懸念された。しかし、商品市況の高止まりが輸出額を押し上げているうえ、重要鉱物を中心とす
る旺盛な需要も輸出を下支えしている。さらに、インフレが加速するなど実質購買力に下押し圧力
がかかるものの、個人消費は底堅く推移するなど、引き続き景気をけん引する動きがみられた。一
方で、プラボウォ大統領の肝煎り政策である学校給食無償プログラムのほか、公務員を対象とする
ボーナス支給効果などが一巡したこと
で政府消費は下振れした。そのうえ、金
融市場の混乱により企業部門の資金調
達環境が厳しさを増すほか、歳出の事実
上削減により公共投資の進捗も低迷し
ており、固定資本投資に下押し圧力がか
かった。固定資本投資の下振れを受けて
輸入も大幅に減少しており、純輸出（輸
出－輸入）の成長率寄与度が前期比年率
ベースで押し上げられた。加えて、在庫
投資の成長率寄与度もプラスに転じる

（https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666784.html）

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
など在庫が積み上がる動きもみられる。景気動向を巡っては、数字と実態の間で乖離が生じている
可能性には注意が必要である（図４）。 
分野ごとの生産動向を巡っても、エネルギー需要の高まりを受けた石炭や天然ガスなどの生産拡
大の動きを反映して、鉱業部門の生産に持ち直しの動きが確認された。さらに、バイオディーゼル
の使用義務化制度が拡充されたことを受けて、パーム油を原料とする燃料生産が拡大しており、製
造業の生産活動も押し上げられた。また、個人消費の堅調な動きを反映して、幅広くサービス業の
生産も底堅い動きをみせている。一方で、公共投資や企業部門の設備投資意欲が後退していること
を受けて、建設業の生産拡大の動きに一服感が出ている。足元では、森林火災による周辺国への煙
害が懸念される状況に直面しているが、異常気象の頻発を受けて農林漁業関連の生産は大きく下振
れしており、先行きは供給減による食料インフレ懸念が高まる可能性に注意する必要がある。

【政府が掲げる意欲的な成長目標実現へ、市場が抱く懸念に着実に対応することが求められる】 
同国政府は、2026 年の経済成長率目標を＋5.4％、中銀は＋4.9～5.7％との見通しを示している
が、９月までの累計ベースの成長率は＋5.45％となり、政府目標をわずかに上回る伸びとなってい
る。政府内においては、スアハシル財務相が 2026 年の経済成長率が目標をわずかに上回る＋5.5％
に達する可能性があるとの見方を示しているが、その可能性が高まっていると捉えられる。ただし、
前述したように、足元の景気動向を巡っては、数字と実態との間の乖離が広がっている可能性が示
唆されることを勘案すれば、先行きも勢いが持続するかは不透明である。さらに、先行きは金融市
場の混乱の影響が顕在化することも考えられることに注意する必要がある。 
プラボウォ大統領は、自身の任期の最終年度である 2029 年までに経済成長率を８％に引き上げ
る目標を掲げている。ここ数年の経済成長率が平均５％程度で推移してきたことを勘案すれば、そ
の実現のためには３pt 程度押し上げる
必要がある。同国は国民の平均年齢が比
較的低いうえ、中期的にも人口増加が見
込まれるなど経済成長への期待は高い
ものの、前述のように金融市場において
は『格下げ』が意識される展開が続いて
いる。こうした状況を反映して、このと
ころは国内・外資本による投資実行額が
頭打ちの様相をみせている（図５）。同国
は近年、経常赤字基調にあり、経済成長
率の押し上げには安定的な海外資金の
流入を促すことが不可欠である。このため、金融市場が抱く懸念に対して、市場改革や構造改革を
通じて着実に対応できるかが重要となっており、プラボウォ政権にとってその課題に取り組む必要
性は一段と高まっている。 
以 上
